45	export function newRun(questionIds: string[]): ConditionalRun {
46	  return { id: crypto.randomUUID(), questionIds, index: 0, drafts: {}, finished: false, elapsed: 0, timerEnabled: false };
47	}
48	function hasCheckedQuestion(run: ConditionalRun, questionId: string, attempts: ConditionalAttempt[]): boolean {
49	  return !!run.drafts[questionId]?.checked || attempts.some(attempt => attempt.questionId === questionId && attempt.id.startsWith(`${run.id}:`));
50	}
51	export function nextUncheckedQuestionIndex(run: ConditionalRun, attempts: ConditionalAttempt[], after = -1): number {
52	  const pending = (id: string) => !hasCheckedQuestion(run, id, attempts);
53	  const next = run.questionIds.findIndex((id, index) => index > after && pending(id));
54	  return next >= 0 ? next : run.questionIds.findIndex(pending);
55	}
56	export function reconcileConditionalRun(run: ConditionalRun, questions: { id: string; unitId: string; difficulty: ConditionalDifficulty }[], attempts: ConditionalAttempt[]): ConditionalRun {
57	  const savedIds = new Set(run.questionIds);
58	  const removedIds = new Set<string>();
59	  const addedIds: string[] = [];
60	  for (const revision of openingQuestionRevisions) {
61	    if (![...revision.previousIds, ...revision.addedIds].some(id => savedIds.has(id))) continue;
62	    const allowedIds = new Set(questions.filter(question => question.unitId === revision.unitId && question.difficulty === revision.difficulty).map(question => question.id));
63	    for (const [retiredId, replacementId] of Object.entries(revision.replacements)) {
64	      if (savedIds.has(retiredId) && allowedIds.has(replacementId)) removedIds.add(retiredId);
65	    }
66	    for (const id of revision.addedIds) if (allowedIds.has(id) && !savedIds.has(id)) addedIds.push(id);
67	  }
68	  if (!removedIds.size && !addedIds.length) return run;
69	  const questionIds = [...run.questionIds.filter(id => !removedIds.has(id)), ...addedIds];
70	  const currentId = run.questionIds[run.index];
71	  const currentIndex = questionIds.indexOf(currentId);
72	  const keepCurrent = !run.finished && currentIndex >= 0 && questions.some(question => question.id === currentId);
73	  const updated = { ...run, questionIds };
74	  const pendingIndex = nextUncheckedQuestionIndex(updated, attempts);
75	  const index = keepCurrent ? currentIndex : pendingIndex >= 0 ? pendingIndex : questionIds.length;
76	  return { ...updated, index, finished: !keepCurrent && pendingIndex < 0 };
77	}
78	export function attemptSummary(attempts: ConditionalAttempt[]) {
79	  return { total: attempts.length, correct: attempts.filter(a => a.correct).length, assisted: attempts.filter(a => a.hint || a.explanationBeforeCheck).length };
80	}
81	export function parseConditionalProgress(value: unknown): ConditionalProgress {
82	  const bad = () => { throw new Error('Your stored conditional progress has an unsupported format. Your record has been preserved; ask Joshua to review it.'); };
83	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return bad();
84	  const record = value as ConditionalProgress;
85	  if (record.id !== 'conditionals-v1' || !record.lessons || typeof record.lessons !== 'object' || !record.runs || typeof record.runs !== 'object' || !Array.isArray(record.attempts) || typeof record.journal !== 'string' || typeof record.settings?.showMethod !== 'boolean' || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(record.updated_at))) return bad();
86	  if (Object.values(record.lessons).some(date => typeof date !== 'string' || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(date)))) return bad();
87	  for (const run of Object.values(record.runs)) {
88	    if (!run || typeof run.id !== 'string' || !Array.isArray(run.questionIds) || run.questionIds.some(id => typeof id !== 'string') || !Number.isInteger(run.index) || run.index < 0 || run.index > run.questionIds.length || !run.drafts || typeof run.drafts !== 'object' || typeof run.finished !== 'boolean' || typeof run.timerEnabled !== 'boolean' || !Number.isFinite(run.elapsed) || run.elapsed < 0 || (run.timerStartedAt != null && !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(run.timerStartedAt)))) return bad();
89	    for (const draft of Object.values(run.drafts)) if (!draft || typeof draft.working !== 'string' || typeof draft.hint !== 'boolean' || typeof draft.revealed !== 'boolean' || typeof draft.checked !== 'boolean' || (draft.choice !== null && (!Number.isInteger(draft.choice) || draft.choice < 0))) return bad();
90	  }
91	  for (const attempt of record.attempts) if (!attempt || typeof attempt.id !== 'string' || typeof attempt.questionId !== 'string' || typeof attempt.unitId !== 'string' || !['beginner', 'medium', 'advanced'].includes(attempt.difficulty) || typeof attempt.correct !== 'boolean' || typeof attempt.hint !== 'boolean' || typeof attempt.explanationBeforeCheck !== 'boolean' || typeof attempt.working !== 'string' || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(attempt.checkedAt)) || (attempt.choice !== null && (!Number.isInteger(attempt.choice) || attempt.choice < 0))) return bad();
92	  return record;
93	}
94	export function elapsedSeconds(run: ConditionalRun, now = Date.now()): number {
95	  return run.elapsed + (run.timerStartedAt ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((now - Date.parse(run.timerStartedAt)) / 1000)) : 0);
96	}
97	export function recommendNext(progress: ConditionalProgress, units: ConditionalUnit[], startingUnitId?: string) {
98	  for (const unit of getCurriculumUnits(units, startingUnitId)) {
99	    const difficulty = getCurriculumDifficulty(unit);
100	    const phase = unit.phase;
101	    if (!progress.lessons[unit.id]) return { mode: 'learn' as const, unitId: unit.id, label: `Learn: ${unit.title}`, difficulty, phase };
102	    if (!progress.attempts.some(attempt => attempt.questionId === unit.guidedExample.id)) return { mode: 'learn' as const, unitId: unit.id, label: `Guided example: ${unit.title}`, difficulty, phase };
103	    if (unit.drills[difficulty].some(question => !progress.attempts.some(attempt => attempt.questionId === question.id && attempt.difficulty === difficulty))) {
104	      return { mode: 'practice' as const, unitId: unit.id, label: `Skill drill: ${unit.title}`, difficulty, phase };
105	    }
106	  }
107	  return null;
108	}

201	type PracticeQuestion = ConditionalQuestion & { unitId: string; unitTitle: string; difficulty: ConditionalDifficulty };
202	function ExpandedLessonReview({ questions, selection }: { questions: PracticeQuestion[]; selection: PathSelection }) {

206	  const unitIds = new Set(questions.map(question => question.unitId));

208	    && needsExpandedConditionalLessonReview(progress.lessons[unit.id])
209	    && (isAdmin || (active && conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(active, { unitId: unit.id, action: 'lesson', difficulty: selection.difficulty }))));
210	  return units.length ? <div className="cb-actions">{units.map(unit => <Link key={unit.id} className="cb-button" to={actionUrl(unit, 'lesson', selection)}>Review expanded lesson: {unit.title}</Link>)}</div> : null;

212	function Practice({ simulator = false, hardLanguage = false }: { simulator?: boolean; hardLanguage?: boolean }) {

216	  const unit = findConditionalUnit(unitId ?? '');

220	  const area = selection.area ?? (hardLanguage ? 'translation' : undefined);

223	  return <><span className="cb-label">{simulator ? 'Mixed application' : 'Skill drill'}</span><h1>{unit ? unit.title : area ? names[area as ConditionalArea] : 'Put your conditional reasoning to work.'}</h1><p>Write or diagram first. Then check the answer and repair your reasoning. Use a hint or worked explanation when you need help.</p>{unit && <UnitActions unit={unit} selection={selection} />}{questions.length ? <div className="cb-card"><QuestionRunner key={`${difficulty}:${unitId ?? area ?? phase}`} runKey={`practice:${difficulty}:${unitId ?? area ?? phase}`} questions={questions} hideSkill={simulator} forwardSelection={{ ...selection, difficulty, area: area as ConditionalArea | undefined, unitId, phase }} /></div> : <div className="cb-card"><p>This practice set is unavailable. Return to your work to open the lesson or activity.</p><Link to={pathUrl(selection)}>Return to your work</Link></div>}</>;

226	function QuestionRunner({ runKey, questions, hideSkill = false, forwardSelection, guidedMethod }: { runKey: string; questions: PracticeQuestion[]; hideSkill?: boolean; forwardSelection?: PathSelection; guidedMethod?: string[] }) {
227	  const { progress, update } = useCourse();
228	  const selection = useSelection();
229	  const run = progress.runs[runKey];
230	  const [timerNow, setTimerNow] = useState(Date.now());
231	  useEffect(() => {
232	    if (run && reconcileConditionalRun(run, questions, progress.attempts) === run) return;
233	    update(previous => {
234	      const savedRun = previous.runs[runKey];
235	      const nextRun = savedRun ? reconcileConditionalRun(savedRun, questions, previous.attempts) : newRun(questions.map(question => question.id));
236	      return nextRun === savedRun ? previous : { ...previous, runs: { ...previous.runs, [runKey]: nextRun } };
237	    });
238	  }, [run, runKey, questions, progress.attempts, update]);
239	  const replaceRun = (transform: (value: ConditionalRun) => ConditionalRun) => update(previous => ({ ...previous, runs: { ...previous.runs, [runKey]: transform(previous.runs[runKey]) } }));
240	  const timerStartedAt = run?.timerStartedAt;
241	  useEffect(() => {
242	    if (!timerStartedAt) return;
243	    const timer = setInterval(() => setTimerNow(Date.now()), 1000);
244	    return () => clearInterval(timer);
245	  }, [timerStartedAt]);
246	  if (!run) return <p>Preparing your practice...</p>;
247	  if (reconcileConditionalRun(run, questions, progress.attempts) !== run) return <p>Preparing your practice...</p>;
248	  const question = questions.find(item => item.id === run.questionIds[run.index]);
249	  if (!question && !run.finished) return <><p>This saved question is not available in this assignment. Your position, written work, and attempts are preserved.</p><Link className="cb-button" to={pathUrl(forwardSelection ?? selection)}>Return to your work</Link></>;
250	  const activeIds = new Set(run.questionIds);
251	  const attempts = progress.attempts.filter(item => item.id.startsWith(`${run.id}:`) && activeIds.has(item.questionId));
252	  const expandedLessonReview = !guidedMethod && <ExpandedLessonReview questions={questions} selection={forwardSelection ?? selection} />;
253	  if (run.finished) {
254	    const summary = attemptSummary(attempts);
255	    return <>{expandedLessonReview}<h2>You finished this set.</h2><p>{summary.correct} correct out of {summary.total} checked answers. {summary.assisted} used a hint or explanation before checking.</p><p>Revisit the reasoning, then continue with the next activity. Finishing a set does not certify mastery.</p><div className="cb-actions"><Link className="cb-button" to={`${BASE}/review${pathUrl(forwardSelection ?? pathSelection(new URLSearchParams())).replace(BASE, '')}`}>Review your attempts</Link><button onClick={() => replaceRun(() => newRun(questions.map(item => item.id)))}><RotateCcw size={16} /> Repeat this set</button></div><RecommendedForward selection={forwardSelection} /></>;
256	  }
257	  const draft = run.drafts[question.id] ?? emptyDraft();
258	  const nextIndex = nextUncheckedQuestionIndex(run, progress.attempts, run.index);
259	  const advance = () => update(previous => {
260	    const currentRun = previous.runs[runKey];
261	    const index = nextUncheckedQuestionIndex(currentRun, previous.attempts, currentRun.index);
262	    return { ...previous, runs: { ...previous.runs, [runKey]: { ...currentRun, index: index < 0 ? currentRun.questionIds.length : index, finished: index < 0, elapsed: elapsedSeconds(currentRun), timerStartedAt: currentRun.timerStartedAt && index >= 0 ? new Date().toISOString() : null } } };
263	  });
264	  const changeDraft = (patch: Partial<AnswerDraft>) => replaceRun(value => ({ ...value, drafts: { ...value.drafts, [question.id]: { ...(value.drafts[question.id] ?? emptyDraft()), ...patch } } }));
265	  const checkAnswer = () => update(previous => {
266	    const currentRun = previous.runs[runKey];
267	    const currentDraft = currentRun.drafts[question.id] ?? emptyDraft();
268	    if (currentDraft.checked || currentDraft.choice === null) return previous;
269	    const attempt = { id: `${currentRun.id}:${question.id}`, questionId: question.id, unitId: question.unitId, difficulty: question.difficulty, choice: currentDraft.choice, correct: currentDraft.choice === question.answerIndex, hint: currentDraft.hint, explanationBeforeCheck: currentDraft.revealed, working: currentDraft.working, checkedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
270	    return { ...previous, attempts: [...previous.attempts, attempt], runs: { ...previous.runs, [runKey]: { ...currentRun, drafts: { ...currentRun.drafts, [question.id]: { ...currentDraft, checked: true, revealed: true } } } } };
271	  });
272	  return <>{expandedLessonReview}<div className="cb-actions"><span className="cb-label">{run.index + 1} / {run.questionIds.length} · {guidedMethod ? 'Guided activity' : "Skill drill"}</span>{question.difficulty !== 'beginner' && <><button onClick={() => { replaceRun(value => ({ ...value, timerEnabled: true })); setTimerNow(Date.now()); replaceRun(value => value.timerStartedAt ? { ...value, elapsed: elapsedSeconds(value), timerStartedAt: null } : { ...value, timerStartedAt: new Date().toISOString() }); }}><Clock size={16} /> {run.timerStartedAt ? 'Pause timing' : 'Start optional timing'}</button>{run.timerEnabled && <span aria-label="Elapsed time">{Math.floor(elapsedSeconds(run, timerNow) / 60)}:{String(elapsedSeconds(run, timerNow) % 60).padStart(2, '0')}</span>}</>}</div>{!hideSkill && <p className="cb-muted">{question.unitTitle}</p>}<h3>{question.prompt}</h3>{guidedMethod && <div className="cb-reason"><h4>Build your response in these steps</h4><ol>{guidedMethod.map(step => <li key={step}>{step}</li>)}</ol><p>Identify the conditions. Draw the relationship. Test what follows, then explain why the tempting alternative fails.</p></div>}<label htmlFor={`working-${question.id}`}>Write your diagram or reasoning first. Arrows such as A → B are welcome.</label><textarea id={`working-${question.id}`} value={draft.working} onChange={event => changeDraft({ working: event.target.value })} placeholder="What is given? What follows? Show the direction and each step." /><p className="cb-muted">Your written work is saved for comparison. It is not automatically graded.</p>{draft.working.trim() || draft.revealed ? <div className="cb-options" role="group" aria-label="Answer choices">{question.options.map((option, index) => <button key={index} aria-pressed={draft.choice === index} disabled={draft.checked} onClick={() => changeDraft({ choice: index })}>{String.fromCharCode(65 + index)}. {option}</button>)}</div> : <p className="cb-muted">Add your reasoning to see the answer choices, or open the explanation if you need help getting started.</p>}<div className="cb-actions"><button onClick={() => changeDraft({ hint: true })}>Get a hint</button><button onClick={() => changeDraft({ revealed: true })}>Show worked explanation</button><button className="cb-primary" disabled={draft.choice === null || draft.checked} onClick={checkAnswer}>{draft.checked ? 'Answer checked' : 'Check answer'}</button></div>{draft.hint && <div className="cb-reason"><strong>Hint: </strong>{question.hint}</div>}{draft.revealed && <div className="cb-result">{draft.checked && <h3>{draft.choice === question.answerIndex ? 'Your choice is correct.' : 'Review the reasoning.'}</h3>}<p><strong>Answer: </strong>{question.options[question.answerIndex]}</p><ol>{question.reasoningSteps.map((step, index) => <li key={index}>{step}</li>)}</ol>{question.explanation !== question.reasoningSteps.join(' ') && <p>{question.explanation}</p>}<p className="cb-muted">Compare your written work with these steps. A matching choice alone does not show whether your diagram was right.</p></div>}{draft.checked && <button className="cb-primary" onClick={advance}>{nextIndex < 0 ? 'Finish set' : 'Next question'} <ArrowRight size={16} /></button>}</>;
273	}

275	function Reference({ indicators = false }: { indicators?: boolean }) {

278	  const units = indicators ? scoped.filter(unit => /group [1-4]/i.test(unit.title)) : scoped;

'An indicator helps identify the relation. Preserve the complete condition and check what the sentence actually says.' : 'Review the method, a worked example, and the mistake to avoid. Open the lesson or practice the exact skill.'}</p>{units.map(unit => <section className="cb-card" key={unit.id}><h2>{unit.title}</h2><p>{unit.goal}</p><ol>{unit.method.map((step, index) => <li key={index}>{step}</li>)}</ol><div className="cb-reason"><p>{unit.workedExample.statements.join(' ')}</p><strong>{unit.workedExample.conclusion}</strong></div><p><strong>Watch for: </strong>{unit.commonMistake.mistake}</p><p>{unit.commonMistake.repair}</p><div className="cb-actions">{!selection.practiceOnly && <Link className="cb-button" to={actionUrl(unit, 'lesson', { ...selection, difficulty: selection.assignedLevel ? selection.difficulty : getCurriculumDifficulty(unit) })}>Full lesson</Link>}<Link className="cb-button" to={actionUrl(unit, 'practice', { ...selection, difficulty: selection.assignedLevel ? selection.difficulty : getCurriculumDifficulty(unit) })}>Practice this skill</Link></div></section>)}</>;
280	}

[...misses].reverse().map(attempt => {

'No choice' : question.options[attempt.choice]}</p><p><strong>Your working: </strong>{attempt.working || 'No written work saved'}</p><div className="cb-result"><strong>Answer: </strong>{question.options[question.answerIndex]}<ol>{question.reasoningSteps.map((step, index) => <li key={index}>{step}</li>)}</ol>{question.explanation !== question.reasoningSteps.join(' ') && <p>{question.explanation}</p>}</div></> : <p>The authored question is unavailable. 3081-+] as const)('reviews the exact historical prompt and saved choice for %s', (id, unitId, difficulty, choice, prompt, selected, answer) => {
3082-+  state.progress!.attempts.push({ ...check(id, unitId, difficulty), choice });
3083-+  mount(`${base}/review`, [course]);
3084-+  const article = screen.getByText(prompt).closest('article')!;
3085-+  expect(within(article).getByText((_text, node) => node?.tagName === 'P' && node.textContent === `Your answer: ${selected}`)).toBeTruthy();
3086-+  expect(within(article).getByText((_text, node) => node?.tagName === 'DIV' && node.className === 'cb-result' && node.textContent?.startsWith(`Answer: ${answer}`))).toBeTruthy();
3087-+  expect(screen.queryByText('The authored question is unavailable. Your attempt remains recorded.')).toBeNull();
3088-+});

243	    goal: "Find the complete sufficient idea in each Group 1 expression and preserve its meaning in a diagram.",
244	    teaching: [
245	      "We've understood enough and required. Now let's see how different words express the sufficient side. Group 1 includes if, when, where, all, the only, every and any in their conditional uses. The words help you find a relationship. You still need to read what the whole sentence says.",
246	      "Start with if: If an order is marked ready, it has been packed. If what? The order is marked ready. That's the sufficient idea. 247	      "When can introduce the event that's enough: When the alarm sounds, the staff evacuate. Ask when what. The alarm sounds. Under this rule, that event guarantees evacuation. 248	      "Where can make a rule about a location or situation: Where a station is available, the service operates. Which locations does the rule cover? Locations with an available station. 249	      "All covers the whole group it names: All registered guests receive a wristband. All what? Registered guests, not guests generally. 304	  {
305	    id: 'group-2', title: "Group 2: Keep the Requirement on the Right", area: 'translation', phase: 'conditional', prerequisites: ["group-1"],
306	    goal: "Find what a Group 2 sentence requires, preserve its complete meaning, and distinguish a requirement from success or compliance.",
307	    teaching: [
308	      "Group 2 helps us find the necessary side: what the sentence says is required. Its core expressions are only, only if, only when, only where, always, requires and must in their relevant uses. We'll keep the complete requirement on the right. First, let's hear why it belongs there.",
309	      "Compare if with only if. An order is marked ready if it has been packed makes packing enough for ready status. An order is marked ready only if it has been packed says packing is required for ready status. Those aren't the same rule. In the second sentence, a packed order could still be waiting for a ready label. The sentence permits that possibility.",
310	      "Only restricts who or what can have the other condition: Only packed orders are marked ready. If an order is marked ready, it must be packed. Only what? Packed orders. Compare The only orders marked ready are packed orders. That Group 1 form reaches the same ready → packed relationship, but its target is orders marked ready. Read the phrase with its grammar rather than treating only and the only as interchangeable labels.",
311	      "Only if introduces a condition that has to be met: A student graduates only if the student completes the project. If the student graduates, the project must be completed. Project completion by itself doesn't promise graduation. Read the reverse aloud and you'll hear the extra guarantee it adds.",
312	      "Only when makes an event or state required: A review occurs only when a complaint is filed. A complaint has to be filed for this review to occur. Imagine a complaint has been filed. Does this sentence say a review now occurs? No. It says what a review requires, not that every complaint gets one.",
313	      "Only where makes a location condition required: The service operates only where a station is available. Wherever the service operates, a station has to be available there. A station at another location isn't enough. Nor has the sentence promised service to every place with an available station.",
314	      "Always describes the result that holds throughout the named cases: A finalist always receives an invitation. Start with a finalist. Under that claim, the person receives an invitation. The subject supplies the sufficient idea; receiving an invitation is the required result. Always belongs in this teaching group, but it doesn't mean the next word automatically goes on the right. Read the complete subject and result.",
315	      "Requires tells you what is needed: Entry requires a current pass. Requires what? A current pass. Entry goes left, having the current pass goes right. Change the wording to A current pass is required for entry. The requirement appears first now, but the relationship hasn't changed. A pass alone isn't a promise that you can enter, and an expired pass doesn't meet the full requirement.",
316	      "Must can state an obligation: A finalist must attend the interview. What does finalist status establish? The person is required to attend. It doesn't establish that the person actually attends; people can fail to meet an obligation. Write Finalist → Required to attend interview so the diagram preserves that difference. Keep permission words such as may in the same careful way.",
317	      "Use the What Test on the full expression, then check with a Personalized If/Then. If my display has activated, what does the battery rule force me to know? Its battery is charged. Now suppose I know only that the battery is charged. The rule still leaves activation open. That comparison checks the arrow against the sentence's ordinary meaning.",
318	      "Keep every part of a requirement. In Entry requires a current pass, pass would drop current. In Only members may use this room, actually uses the room would change permission into an action. The right direction can't repair a label that has lost part of the idea.",
319	      "In the fresh practice, explain what is required and what remains open. If your answer treats a requirement as enough, try saying that answer as an if/then. Compare it with the original sentence and point to the extra promise. That's the mistake we're learning to catch.",
320	    ],

325	      "Place the restricted event or condition left and its requirement right. Check that direction with a Personalized If/Then.",

3	export interface ConditionalAssignmentTarget {

5	  mode: 'learn' | 'practice';
6	  difficulty: ConditionalDifficulty;
7	  unitId?: string;

9	  /** Unmarked stage grants keep their original membership. New grants use this teaching order. */

12	export const DEFAULT_CONDITIONAL_TARGET: ConditionalAssignmentTarget = {
13	  scope: 'course', mode: 'learn', difficulty: 'beginner',
14	};
15	export const CONDITIONAL_AREAS: { id: ConditionalArea; title: string }[] = [
16	  { id: 'understanding', title: 'Understanding rules' },
17	  { id: 'translation', title: 'Translating language' },
18	  { id: 'linking', title: 'Connecting rules' },
19	  { id: 'inference', title: 'Using rules with facts' },
20	  { id: 'complex', title: 'Complex conditionals' },
21	  { id: 'application', title: 'Logical Reasoning applications' },
22	  { id: 'quantifiers', title: 'Quantifier reasoning' },
23	];
24	export function conditionalTargetKey(target: ConditionalAssignmentTarget = DEFAULT_CONDITIONAL_TARGET): string {
25	  if (target.scope === 'course') return 'course';
26	  if (target.scope === 'stage') return target.curriculumVersion === 2 ? `stage:v2:${target.unitId}` : `stage:${target.unitId}`;
27	  return [target.scope, target.scope === 'skill' ? target.unitId : target.scope === 'area' ? target.area : 'conditional',
28	    target.scope === 'general' ? 'practice' : target.mode, target.difficulty].join(':');
29	}
30	export async function resolveConditionalAssignment(target: ConditionalAssignmentTarget = DEFAULT_CONDITIONAL_TARGET) {
31	  if (!['course', 'stage', 'area', 'skill', 'general'].includes(target.scope) ||
32	      !['learn', 'practice'].includes(target.mode) ||
33	      !['beginner', 'medium', 'advanced'].includes(target.difficulty) ||
34	      (target.curriculumVersion !== undefined && target.curriculumVersion !== 2)) {
35	    throw new Error('Choose a valid conditional scope, learning mode and difficulty.');
36	  }
37	  const base = '/bootcamp/conditionals';
38	  if (target.scope === 'course') return {
39	    route: base, title: 'Conditional Logic Bootcamp',
40	    description: 'The teaching sequence from the beginning, with activity levels set at each stage. Quantifiers follow conditionals.',
41	    target: { ...DEFAULT_CONDITIONAL_TARGET }, key: 'course',
42	  };
43	  if (target.scope === 'stage') {
44	    const { findConditionalUnit } = await import('@/bootcamps/conditionals/content');
45	    const unit = findConditionalUnit(target.unitId);
46	    if (!unit) throw new Error('Choose an existing conditional starting stage.');
47	    const normalized: ConditionalAssignmentTarget = { scope: 'stage', mode: 'learn', difficulty: 'beginner', unitId: unit.id,
48	      ...(target.curriculumVersion === 2 ? { curriculumVersion: 2 } : {}) };
49	    return {
50	      route: `${base}?${new URLSearchParams({ start: unit.id })}`,
51	      title: `Conditional curriculum: start with ${unit.title}`,
52	      description: 'Start at the assigned skill and continue through the curriculum. Each activity has its own level.',
53	      target: normalized, key: conditionalTargetKey(normalized),
54	    };
55	  }
56	  const params = new URLSearchParams({ level: target.difficulty });
57	  let label = 'General conditional practice';
58	  if (target.scope === 'skill') {
59	    const { findConditionalUnit } = await import('@/bootcamps/conditionals/content');
60	    const unit = findConditionalUnit(target.unitId);
61	    if (!unit) throw new Error('Choose an existing conditional skill.');
62	    label = unit.title;
63	    params.set('unit', unit.id);
64	  } else if (target.scope === 'area') {
65	    const area = CONDITIONAL_AREAS.find(option => option.id === target.area);
66	    if (!area) throw new Error('Choose an existing conditional area.');
67	    label = area.title;
68	    params.set('area', area.id);
69	  } else params.set('phase', 'conditional');
70	  const mode = target.scope === 'general' ? 'practice' : target.mode;
71	  const route = mode === 'practice' ? `${base}/practice?${params}` : target.scope === 'skill'
72	    ? `${base}/lessons/${encodeURIComponent(target.unitId!)}?${params}` : `${base}?${params}`;
73	  return {
74	    route, title: `${label}: ${mode === 'learn' ? 'learning and practice' : 'practice'}`,
75	    description: mode === 'learn' ? 'Teaching, guided activities and practice.' : 'Practice with explanations and optional hints.',
76	    target: { scope: target.scope, mode, difficulty: target.difficulty,
77	      ...(target.scope === 'skill' ? { unitId: target.unitId } : {}), ...(target.scope === 'area' ? { area: target.area } : {}) } as ConditionalAssignmentTarget,
78	    key: conditionalTargetKey({ ...target, mode }),
79	  };
80	}
81	
82	/** Normalize only exact former generated copy; teacher-written wording stays intact. */
83	export function conditionalAssignmentDisplayCopy(
84	  target: ConditionalAssignmentTarget,
85	  generated: { title: string; description: string },
86	  stored: { title?: string | null; description?: string | null },
87	) {
88	  let previousTitle: string | undefined;
89	  let previousDescription: string | undefined;
90	  if (['skill', 'area', 'general'].includes(target.scope)) {
91	    const mode = target.scope === 'general' ? 'practice' : target.mode;
92	    const suffix = mode === 'learn' ? 'learning and practice' : 'practice';
93	    const label = generated.title.slice(0, -(`: ${suffix}`.length));
94	    const level = `Level ${['beginner', 'medium', 'advanced'].indexOf(target.difficulty) + 1}`;
95	    previousTitle = `${label}: ${level} ${suffix}`;
96	    previousDescription = mode === 'learn'
97	      ? `Teaching, guided activities and assigned ${level.toLowerCase()} practice.`
98	      : `Assigned ${level.toLowerCase()} practice with explanations and optional hints.`;
99	  }
100	  return {
101	    title: !stored.title || stored.title === previousTitle ? generated.title : stored.title,
102	    description: !stored.description || stored.description === previousDescription ? generated.description : stored.description,
103	  };
104	}

1	import { conditionalUnits, findConditionalUnit } from './content';
2	import { DIFFICULTIES, type ConditionalDifficulty } from './content/types';
3	import { getCurriculumDifficulty, getCurriculumUnits } from './curriculumPath';
4	import { CONDITIONAL_AREAS, DEFAULT_CONDITIONAL_TARGET, conditionalAssignmentDisplayCopy, resolveConditionalAssignment, type ConditionalAssignmentTarget } from '@/lib/conditionalBootcampAssignment';
5	import { classroomItemMatchesBootcamp } from '@/lib/bootcamps';
6	
7	export interface ConditionalAssignmentRow {
8	  id: string; student_id: string; type: string; title: string; description?: string | null;
9	  source_id: string | null; metadata_json: Record<string, unknown> | null;
10	  status: string; visible_to_student: boolean; unlock_at: string | null;
11	}
12	export interface ResolvedConditionalAssignment {
13	  id: string; title: string; description?: string; route: string; target: ConditionalAssignmentTarget;
14	  unitIds: string[]; mode: 'learn' | 'practice'; difficulty?: ConditionalDifficulty;
15	}
16	function parseTarget(value: unknown): ConditionalAssignmentTarget | null {
17	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return null;
18	  const target = value as ConditionalAssignmentTarget;
19	  if (!['course', 'stage', 'area', 'skill', 'general'].includes(target.scope) ||
20	    !['learn', 'practice'].includes(target.mode) || !DIFFICULTIES.includes(target.difficulty) ||
21	    (target.curriculumVersion !== undefined && target.curriculumVersion !== 2)) return null;
22	  if ((target.scope === 'skill' || target.scope === 'stage') && !findConditionalUnit(target.unitId)) return null;
23	  if (target.scope === 'area' && !CONDITIONAL_AREAS.some(area => area.id === target.area)) return null;
24	  return target;
25	}
26	function legacyTarget(source: string | null): ConditionalAssignmentTarget | null {
27	  if (!source || !source.startsWith('/bootcamp/conditionals')) return null;
28	  const url = new URL(source, 'https://local.invalid');
29	  const base = '/bootcamp/conditionals';
30	  if (url.pathname !== base && !url.pathname.startsWith(`${base}/`)) return null;
31	  const level = url.searchParams.get('level') ?? 'beginner';
32	  if (!DIFFICULTIES.includes(level as ConditionalDifficulty)) return null;
33	  const difficulty = level as ConditionalDifficulty;
34	  const start = url.searchParams.get('start');
35	  if (start) return parseTarget({ scope: 'stage', mode: 'learn', difficulty, unitId: start });
36	  const pathUnit = url.pathname.match(/^\/bootcamp\/conditionals\/(?:lessons|activities)\/([^/]+)$/)?.[1];
37	  const unitId = pathUnit ?? url.searchParams.get('unit');
38	  const area = url.searchParams.get('area');
39	  const mode = url.pathname === `${base}/practice` ? 'practice' : 'learn';
40	  if (unitId) return parseTarget({ scope: 'skill', mode, difficulty, unitId });
41	  if (area) return parseTarget({ scope: 'area', mode, difficulty, area });
42	  if (url.pathname === `${base}/practice`) return parseTarget({ scope: 'general', mode: 'practice', difficulty });
43	  return url.pathname === base && !url.search ? { ...DEFAULT_CONDITIONAL_TARGET } : null;
44	}
45	export function conditionalAssignmentUnitIds(target: ConditionalAssignmentTarget): string[] {
46	  if (target.scope === 'course') return getCurriculumUnits(conditionalUnits).map(unit => unit.id);
47	  if (target.scope === 'stage') {
48	    if (target.curriculumVersion === 2) return getCurriculumUnits(conditionalUnits, target.unitId).map(unit => unit.id);
49	    // Existing stage grants refer to the original membership, not a mutable display order.
50	    const originalOpening = ['orientation', 'rules-facts', 'sufficient-necessary', 'truth-validity', 'negation', 'contraposition', 'group-1', 'group-2'];
51	    const originalIds = [...originalOpening, ...conditionalUnits.map(unit => unit.id).filter(id => !originalOpening.includes(id))];
52	    const start = originalIds.indexOf(target.unitId);
53	    return originalIds.slice(Math.max(0, start));
54	  }
55	  return conditionalUnits.filter(unit => target.scope === 'skill' ? unit.id === target.unitId
56	    : target.scope === 'area' ? unit.area === target.area : unit.phase === 'conditional').map(unit => unit.id);
57	}
58	export async function resolveConditionalAssignments(rows: ConditionalAssignmentRow[], userId: string, now = Date.now()): Promise<ResolvedConditionalAssignment[]> {
59	  const result: ResolvedConditionalAssignment[] = [];
60	  for (const row of rows) {
61	    if (row.student_id !== userId || row.type !== 'bootcamp_module' || !row.visible_to_student ||
62	      !['assigned', 'in_progress', 'completed'].includes(row.status) || !classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(row, 'conditionals')) continue;
63	    if (row.unlock_at && (!Number.isFinite(Date.parse(row.unlock_at)) || Date.parse(row.unlock_at) > now)) continue;
64	    const metadata = row.metadata_json;
65	    const target = metadata && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(metadata, 'conditional_target')
66	      ? parseTarget(metadata.conditional_target) : legacyTarget(row.source_id);
67	    if (!target) continue;
68	    const resolved = await resolveConditionalAssignment(target);
69	    result.push({ id: row.id, ...conditionalAssignmentDisplayCopy(resolved.target, resolved, row), route: resolved.route, target: resolved.target,
70	      unitIds: conditionalAssignmentUnitIds(resolved.target), mode: resolved.target.mode,
71	      ...(target.scope !== 'course' && target.scope !== 'stage' ? { difficulty: resolved.target.difficulty } : {}) });
72	  }
73	  return result;
74	}
75	/** URL parameters never grant work. Call this against a target read from the student's assignment row. */
76	export function conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(assignment: ResolvedConditionalAssignment, request: {
77	  unitId: string; action: 'lesson' | 'guided' | 'practice'; difficulty?: ConditionalDifficulty;
78	}): boolean {
79	  if (!assignment.unitIds.includes(request.unitId)) return false;
80	  if (request.action !== 'practice') return assignment.mode === 'learn';
81	  return request.difficulty === (assignment.difficulty ?? getCurriculumDifficulty(request.unitId));
82	}
83	export async function conditionalTeacherPreview(): Promise<ResolvedConditionalAssignment> {
84	  const resolved = await resolveConditionalAssignment();
85	  return { id: 'teacher-preview', title: resolved.title, route: resolved.route, target: resolved.target,
86	    unitIds: conditionalAssignmentUnitIds(resolved.target), mode: 'learn' };
87	}

11	    { question: 'Why do conditionals matter when evaluating an argument?', answer: 'They can supply the connection that makes a conclusion follow. They can also show where an author needs more information. If the argument uses a rule backward, check whether anything actually gave it that additional direction.' },
12	  ],
13	  'sufficient-necessary': [
14	    { question: 'What does sufficient mean?', answer: 'Enough to establish the other condition under this rule. Once the sufficient condition is met, the rule gives you the necessary condition. Enough does not mean it is the only possible way for the other condition to be true.' },
15	    { question: 'Every order marked ready has been packed. Does packing establish the ready label?', answer: 'No. Packing is required for every ready order, but the sentence has not made packing enough for that label. A packed order waiting to be marked ready fits the rule. We need more information to settle its ready status.' },
16	    { question: 'What does necessary mean?', answer: 'Required whenever the sufficient condition holds. Ask required for what, and read the complete relationship. In Marked ready → Packed, packing is required for every ready order; those roles belong to this particular rule.' },
17	    { question: 'If a requirement is met, does that prove the other condition happened?', answer: 'No. A requirement may be present while the other condition remains unknown. Entry requires a pass, but a person could have a pass and stay outside. The rule has not turned the pass into a guarantee of entry.' },
18	  ],
19	  'group-1': [
20	    { question: 'A student receives feedback if the student revises the essay. Write the rule.', answer: 'Revises essay → Receives feedback. If refers to revising, even though that clause appears last. Knowing the essay was revised gives you feedback under this rule; feedback alone does not establish that the student revised.' },
21	    { question: 'Any applicant who submits late pays a fee. Why is “Applicant → Pays fee” too broad?', answer: 'It drops the late-submission restriction. Applicant alone would include someone who submitted on time, whom the sentence does not cover. Keep the complete rule: Applicant submits late → Pays fee.' },
22	    { question: 'Every student who registers early receives a discount. Write the rule.', answer: 'Student registers early → Receives discount. Every refers to students who register early. Early registration is part of the sufficient condition, so keep it when you shorten the idea.' },
23	    { question: 'A fee is paid by any applicant who submits late. Write the rule.', answer: 'Applicant submits late → Pays fee. Any still refers to late-submitting applicants. Moving that phrase later does not change its sufficient role. Read the arrow as: if an applicant submits late, that applicant pays a fee.' },
24	  ],
25	  'group-2': [
26	    { question: 'A permit is required for construction. Write the rule.', answer: 'Construction → Has permit. What is required? The permit, even though it appears before required. The sentence makes it necessary for construction, not enough to establish that construction occurs.' },
27	    { question: 'Why not automatically take the words immediately after the indicator?', answer: 'The complete target can appear before or after the expression. In a permit is required for construction, the permit is the requirement. Ask what the indicator refers to, then check your diagram against the meaning of the whole sentence.' },
28	    { question: 'A finalist must attend the interview. Maya attended. Does that prove she is a finalist?', answer: 'No. The rule gives finalists an obligation; it does not say only finalists attend. Actual attendance is also different from being required to attend. The fact about Maya does not establish finalist status.' },
29	    { question: 'A garden light switches on only when its sensor detects darkness. Write the rule.', answer: 'Light switches on → Sensor detects darkness. Only when makes darkness detection required for switching on. The sentence has not said detection is enough, so knowing the sensor detects darkness alone would leave the light unknown.' },
30	  ],
31	};

33	function Recall({ items }: { items: Card[] }) {
34	  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
35	  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState(false);
36	  const answerId = useId();
37	  const item = items[index];
38	  return <div className="co-flash">
39	    <p className="co-kicker">Review · {index + 1} of {items.length}</p>
40	    <div className={`co-flash-face ${revealed ? 'co-flipped' : ''}`}>
41	      <strong>{item.question}</strong><span>Answer in your own words, then compare.</span>
42	      <div id={answerId} aria-live="polite">{revealed && <p>{item.answer}</p>}</div>
43	    </div>
44	    <div className="co-choices">
45	      <button type="button" aria-expanded={revealed} aria-controls={answerId} onClick={() => setRevealed(!revealed)}>{revealed ? 'Hide recall explanation' : 'Reveal recall explanation'}</button>
46	      <button type="button" disabled={index === 0} onClick={() => { setIndex(index - 1); setRevealed(false); }}>Previous example</button>
47	      <button type="button" onClick={() => { setIndex((index + 1) % items.length); setRevealed(false); }}>{index === items.length - 1 ? 'Start these again' : 'Next example'}</button>
48	    </div>
49	  </div>;
50	}
51	
52	export function ConditionalOpeningMedia({ unitId }: { unitId: string }) {
53	  const items = cards[unitId];
54	  if (!items) return null;
55	  return <section id="co-return-to-the-relationship" className="co-section" data-toc data-toc-title="Return to the relationship">
56	    <h2>Return to the relationship</h2>
57	    <p>Try these before moving on. Say what the rule gives you and explain why.</p>
58	    <Recall key={unitId} items={items}/>
59	    {unitId === 'sufficient-necessary' && <details className="co-media-summary">
60	      <summary>See the relationships together</summary>
61	      <figure className="co-relationship-overview">
62	        <h3>Every order marked ready has been packed.</h3>
63	        <div className="co-overview-roles"><div><strong>Marked ready</strong><span>Sufficient: enough</span></div><b aria-hidden="true">→</b><span className="co-sr-only">implies</span><div><strong>Packed</strong><span>Necessary: required</span></div></div>
64	        <div className="co-overview-set">
65	          <svg viewBox="0 0 600 250" role="img" aria-labelledby="co-order-overview-title co-order-overview-desc">
66	            <title id="co-order-overview-title">Marked ready is sufficient. Packed is necessary.</title>
67	            <desc id="co-order-overview-desc">The ready group is contained within packed orders. All ready orders are packed. Packed alone does not establish the ready label. The drawing does not require unequal group sizes.</desc>
68	            <rect x="10" y="10" width="580" height="230" rx="24" fill="#d7e9df"/>
69	            <rect x="40" y="78" width="285" height="132" rx="18" fill="#fcf3df" stroke="#bd8a47"/>
70	          </svg>
71	          <span className="co-overview-packed-label">Packed orders</span><span className="co-overview-ready-label">Orders marked ready</span>
72	        </div>
73	        <p>Packed alone: the ready label remains unknown.</p>
74	        <figcaption>Read the arrow in its stated direction. An order can be packed before the ready label is added.</figcaption>
75	      </figure>
76	    </details>}
77	  </section>;
78	}

17	function sectionTitle(unit: ConditionalUnit, index: number): string {
18	  if (unit.id === 'orientation') return ['What a conditional says', 'A rule needs a case fact', 'Keep the strength of the claim'][index] || 'Read the complete guarantee';
19	  if (unit.id === 'rules-facts') return ['Separate the fact and rule', 'What each fact can establish', 'A chain produces a new rule', 'Show the match and explain why', 'Four facts, one unchanged rule', 'A requirement is not a completed action'][index] || 'Check the whole claim';
20	  if (unit.area === 'translation') return ['Read the indicator in context', 'Preserve the complete ideas', 'Check the meaning before shortening', 'Use a meaning check', 'Practice the translation', 'Recognize related expressions', 'Keep the direction precise'][index] || 'Compare the wording';
21	  if (unit.area === 'linking') return ['Find the exact connection', 'Keep rules separate from facts', 'Preserve subjects and scope', 'Expose a hidden connection', 'Make the path visible', 'Check intermediate conclusions', 'Match the complete requirement'][index] || 'Test the proposed connection';
22	  return index === 0 ? 'Understand the relationship' : index === 1 ? 'Compare the possible cases' : index === 2 ? 'Keep the whole claim' : `Check the reasoning ${index - 1}`;
23	}
24	
25	export function ConditionalLessonNote({ unit, onComplete, continueHref, continueLabel, previousHref, reviewed }: ConditionalLessonNoteProps) {
26	  if (hasOpeningLesson(unit.id)) return <ConditionalOpeningLesson key={unit.id} unit={unit} onComplete={onComplete} continueHref={continueHref} continueLabel={continueLabel} previousHref={previousHref} reviewed={reviewed} />;
27	  return <div className="conditional-note">
28	    <DynamicIslandTOC key={unit.id} selector=".conditional-note [data-toc]" />
29	    <header className="cn-hero"><div><p className="cn-label">Conditional logic</p><h1>{unit.title}</h1><p>{unit.goal}</p></div><a className="cn-start" href="#conditional-note-start">Read the lesson<span aria-hidden="true">↓</span></a></header>
30	    <article id="conditional-note-start" className="cn-paper">
31	      {unit.id === 'orientation' && <section data-toc data-toc-title="A promise about qualifying cases" className="cn-section"><p className="cn-label">Start with the meaning</p><h2>A promise about qualifying cases</h2><p>A conditional connects two complete ideas. It says: if the starting condition is met, the required result must follow. It does not say that the starting condition has already happened.</p><div className="cn-diagram"><span>If this condition holds</span><span aria-hidden="true">→</span><span>This result must follow</span></div><p>The left side is the trigger. The right side is what the rule guarantees. Understand that promise before memorizing indicator words.</p></section>}
32	      {unit.teaching.map((paragraph, index) => <section key={index} className="cn-section" data-toc data-toc-title={sectionTitle(unit, index)}><p className="cn-label">{String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')}</p><h2>{sectionTitle(unit, index)}</h2><p>{paragraph}</p></section>)}
33	      <section className="cn-section" data-toc data-toc-title="Work through an example"><h2>Work through an example</h2><div className="cn-example">{unit.workedExample.statements.map((statement, index) => <p key={index}>{statement}</p>)}</div><ol className="cn-reasoning">{unit.workedExample.reasoning.map((reason, index) => <li key={index}>{reason}</li>)}</ol><div className="cn-result"><p className="cn-label">What follows</p><p><strong>{unit.workedExample.conclusion}</strong></p></div></section>
34	      <section className="cn-section" data-toc data-toc-title="Try the relationship"><h2>Try the relationship</h2><LessonInteraction unit={unit} /></section>
35	      <section className="cn-section" data-toc data-toc-title="A method to check your work"><h2>A method to check your work</h2><ol className="cn-method">{unit.method.map((step, index) => <li key={index}>{step}</li>)}</ol></section>
36	      <section id="repair" className="cn-section" data-toc data-toc-title="Catch the tempting mistake"><h2>Catch the tempting mistake</h2><div className="cn-contrast"><div><p className="cn-label">The tempting move</p><p>{unit.commonMistake.mistake}</p></div><div><p className="cn-label">Check it this way</p><p>{unit.commonMistake.repair}</p></div></div></section>
37	      <footer className="cn-footer"><p>{reviewed ? 'You marked this lesson as read. You can revisit it whenever you need.' : 'Marking this lesson as read records review, not mastery.'}</p><div className="cn-controls">{previousHref && <Link to={previousHref}>Previous lesson</Link>}<button type="button" onClick={onComplete}>{reviewed ? 'Lesson read' : 'Mark lesson as read'}</button><Link className="cn-primary" to={continueHref}>Continue to practice →</Link></div></footer>
38	    </article>
39	  </div>;
